- **interview-me**: Extracts what the user actually wants instead of what they think they should want, through a one-question-at-a-time interview until ~95% confidence about the underlying intent. - **interview-me-zh**: 需求访谈、意图澄清、把"想要的"和"以为该要的"分开。当用户说「采访我」「帮我问清楚我到底要什么」「先别做，确认一下」「我说的是仪表盘但可能不是」，或者一个需求缺少「给谁用 / 为什么现在 / 成功长什么样 / 硬约束是什么」中的任何一项，或你发现自己在默默补全模糊需求时使用。方法：先写一句话假设 + 置信度；一次只问一个问题并附上自己的猜测；识别"听起来正确"的套话；用对方的话复述（结果/用户/为何现在/成功/约束/范围外）；只有明确的"是"才算确认；能预测接下来三个问题的答案就停。改编自 addyosmani/agent-skills 的 interview-me（MIT）。与「需求盘问官」互补：那个按设计树批量问，这个一次一问、带猜测。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/interview-me-zh))

- **invariant-analysis**: Auto-loaded by logic-auditor agent during Phase 2 for invariant extraction. Framework: "What conditions must ALWAYS be true? How can they be violated?" - **invariant-analysis**: Use when enumerating security claims from a design, formalizing them as invariants, checking for hidden assumptions, and assessing verification feasibility. ([dtsong/agentic-council](https://github.com/dtsong/agentic-council/tree/HEAD/skills/prover-invariant-analysis))
- **invariant-analyzer**: Identify and verify loop invariants for correctness proofs ([a5c-ai/babysitter](https://github.com/a5c-ai/babysitter/tree/HEAD/library/specializations/algorithms-optimization/skills/invariant-analyzer) · ★1.8k)
- **invariant-analyzer**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/invariant-analyzer) · ★729)
- **invariant-audit**: Audit a code change to the ipax interior-point solver against its five non-negotiable architectural invariants, its math/citation conventions, its GPU/device-efficiency rules, and its testing discipline. Use this whenever reviewing a diff that touches anything under ipax/ (the core solver package) — it catches semantic violations that lint/type/purity checks cannot see, such as a NumPy-only attribute slipping through an Array-API-generic code path, or a concrete solve hard-wired into the iteration loop. ([wahln/ipax](https://github.com/wahln/ipax/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/invariant-audit) · ★4)
- **invariant-audit**: Use when asked to run the invariant audit, audit the invariants, audit this diff or branch, or before merging an effort. Exports the current branch diff with audit-diff.mjs, dispatches the read-only machinery:invariant-auditor agent on it, relays findings unchanged, and requires each dispositioned before merge. ([gnydick/ai-skills](https://github.com/gnydick/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/docs/parked/for-unbreakable/skill-source/invariant-audit))
- **invariant-audit**: Audit recent changes (staged, uncommitted, or last N commits) for violations of PocketScribe's hard invariants. Fast guardrail before commit. ([pokt-network/pocket-scribe](https://github.com/pokt-network/pocket-scribe/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/invariant-audit))
- **invariant-audit**: Adversarially sweep a repo's standing invariants (billing/idempotency, PII, published contracts, deploy order) across every execution path a diff touches — retry, redelivery, timer, error, concurrency, rollout — not just the happy path. Use before merging changes that touch money, external calls, PII, published contracts, or workflow/state-machine definitions. Skip for docs-only diffs and refactors that add no execution paths. ([Tsurai7/agentic-playbook](https://github.com/Tsurai7/agentic-playbook/tree/HEAD/skills/invariant-audit))
